<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DfCmXhdkYWR">Pumpkin Wednesday 🎃 Show me your pumpkin. Pumpkin patches, porch carvings, jack-o-lanterns - festive or full-on spooky, all of it counts. A carved pumpkin face keeping watch over the Stocker Farms sign, corn on both sides. ONE photo. Replying is consent: 3 favorites featured tomorrow in a Threads post and an Instagram reel, pinned to the profile, with full credit. Standout replies may also appear in an occasional best-of-the-week reel. What does pumpkin season look like where you are?</a></h3>
